00210: def meshes(name):
00211:     root=bpy.data.objects.get(name)
00212:     assert root is not None, 'Missing semantic marker '+name
00213:     result=[o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH']
00214:     if root.type=='MESH': result.append(root)
00215:     assert result, 'No meshes beneath '+name
00216:     return root,result
00217: 
00218: def material_for(name,label):
00219:     root,objects=meshes(name)
00220:     m=bpy.data.materials.new(label)
00221:     m.use_nodes=True
00222:     for obj in objects:
00223:         obj.data.materials.clear()
00224:         obj.data.materials.append(m)
00225:     return root,m

00212: def meshes(name):
00213:     root=bpy.data.objects.get(name)
00214:     assert root is not None,name
00215:     obs=[o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH']
00216:     if root.type=='MESH': obs.append(root)
00217:     assert obs,name

00186: def semantic_meshes(name):
00187:     roots=[o for o in bpy.data.objects if o.type=='EMPTY'
00188:            and (o.name==name or o.name.startswith(name+'.'))]
00189:     assert roots,'Missing semantic marker: '+name

00191:     for root in roots:
00192:         meshes.update(o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH')
00193:     assert meshes,'Empty target: '+name
00194:     return roots[0],list(meshes)

00193: def semantic_meshes(name):
00194:     roots = [o for o in bpy.data.objects if o.type == 'EMPTY' and
00195:              (o.name == name or o.name.startswith(name + '.'))]
00196:     assert roots, 'Missing semantic marker: ' + name
00197:     meshes = set()
00198:     for root in roots:
00199:         meshes.update(o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH')
00200:     assert meshes, 'Empty material target: ' + name
00201:     return roots[0], list(meshes)
00202: 
00203: def new_material(name, color, roughness=.5, metallic=0):
00204:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00205:     mat.use_nodes = True
00206:     bs = mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00207:     bs.inputs['Base Color'].default_value = (*color,1)
00208:     bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = roughness
00209:     bs.inputs['Metallic'].default_value = metallic
00210:     return mat
00211: 
00212: def assign(name, mat):
00213:     root, meshes = semantic_meshes(name)
00214:     for ob in meshes:
00215:         ob.data.materials.clear()
00216:         ob.data.materials.append(mat)
00217:     return root, meshes

00242: def semantic_meshes(name):
00243:     roots = [o for o in bpy.data.objects if o.type == 'EMPTY'
00244:              and (o.name == name or o.name.startswith(name+'.'))]
00245:     assert roots, 'Missing semantic marker: '+name
00246:     meshes = set()
00247:     for root in roots:
00248:         meshes.update(o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH')
00249:     assert meshes, 'Empty material target: '+name
00250:     return roots[0], list(meshes)
00251: 
00252: def material(name, color, roughness=.5, metallic=0):
00253:     mat = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00254:     mat.use_nodes = True
00255:     bs = mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00256:     bs.inputs['Base Color'].default_value = (*color,1)
00257:     bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = roughness
00258:     bs.inputs['Metallic'].default_value = metallic
00259:     return mat
00260: 
00261: def assign(name, mat):
00262:     root, meshes = semantic_meshes(name)
00263:     for ob in meshes:
00264:         ob.data.materials.clear()
00265:         ob.data.materials.append(mat)
00266:     return root, meshes

00186: def semantic_meshes(name):
00187:     roots=[o for o in bpy.data.objects if o.type=='EMPTY'
00188:            and (o.name==name or o.name.startswith(name+'.'))]
00189:     assert roots,'Missing semantic marker: '+name
00190:     meshes=set()
00191:     for root in roots:
00192:         meshes.update(o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type=='MESH')
00193:     assert meshes,'Empty target: '+name
00194:     return roots[0],list(meshes)

00196: def material(name,color,roughness=.5,metallic=0):
00197:     mat=bpy.data.materials.new(name)
00198:     mat.use_nodes=True
00199:     bs=mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00200:     bs.inputs['Base Color'].default_value=(*color,1)
00201:     bs.inputs['Roughness'].default_value=roughness
00202:     bs.inputs['Metallic'].default_value=metallic

00133: def detailbox(center,size,c,angle=0):
00134:     x,y,z=center
00135:     a,b,d=[s/2 for s in size]
00136:     ca,sa=math.cos(angle),math.sin(angle)
00137:     verts=[]
00138:     for zz in [-d,d]:
00139:         for xx,yy in [(-a,-b),(a,-b),(a,b),(-a,b)]:

00144: def cylinder(a,b,r,c,n=12,r2=None):
00145:     a,b=Vector(a),Vector(b)
00146:     axis=(b-a).normalized()
00147:     ref=Vector((0,0,1)) if abs(axis.z)<.9 else Vector((0,1,0))
00148:     u=axis.cross(ref).normalized()
00149:     v=axis.cross(u).normalized()
00150:     rr=r if r2 is None else r2
00151:     verts=[]
00152:     for p,rad in [(a,r),(b,rr)]:
00153:         for i in range(n):
00154:             t=i*2*math.pi/n
00155:             verts.append(tuple(p+rad*(math.cos(t)*u+math.sin(t)*v)))
00156:     faces=[tuple(reversed(range(n))),tuple(range(n,2*n))]
00157:     faces.extend([(i,(i+1)%n,(i+1)%n+n,i+n) for i in range(n)])
00158:     addpoly(c,verts,faces)

00006: # Inventory: cream upholstered chaise, carved sage-and-gold wooden frame,
00007: # four curved feet, raised head and arms, one loose ivory pillow, green label.
00008: # Architecture: pale paneled rear wall, right pilaster, dark parquet floor,
00009: # bright window and hanging curtains at left. A cane chair is cropped foreground.
00010: # Arbitrary scale: chaise length about 2.35 units. Hidden construction is hypothetical.
00011: # Near/far order: foreground chair, chaise front rail, seat/pillow, head, wall.
00012: # Camera and furniture parameters are independent; no calibration is assumed.

00014: scene = Scene('Paneled room with chaise')
00015: L, W = 2.35, .94

00017: wood = (.34, .39, .30)

00023: def tube(parent, key, pts, radius, color):
00024:     parent.include(key, sweep_tube(points=pts, radius=radius, color=color))
00025: 
00026: def beam(parent, key, size, center, color):
00027:     parent.include(key, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))

00031:                            color=(.20, .105, .06)))

00039:         board = box((.60, .142, .012), center=(x, y, -.012), color=col)
